Storytel

http://www.purl.org/storytel/
imports
http://cidoc-crm.org/extensions/lrmoo/owl/1.0/
http://www.cidoc-crm.org/cidoc-crm/owl/7.1.3/
http://www.cidoc-crm.org/extensions/crmdig/owl/5.0/
Title
Storytel
Description
Storytel is the ontology of the european funded project Storytel. Its goal is to map the relationship between Full Video, Anonymized Video and Anonymized Story re-using existing ontologies and implementing some ad-hoc classes.
Creator
Giorgia Crosilla
Language
English
Other visualisation
WebVOWL Source
Export
Turtle RDF/XML JSON-LD N-Triples

Acoustic Annotation
c

back to ToC or Class ToC

IRI: http://www.purl.org/storytel/Acoustic_Annotation

comment
Annotation holding the result of an acoustic analysis (Praat) of a segment of the Story. It is computed on the original, non-anonymized audio, so it may contain speaker-identifying information and should be handled according to the project data-protection policy.
en
is disjoint with
Textual Annotationc
is defined by
http://www.purl.org/storytel/
is sub class of
Annotationc
l43 annotatesop
some
Anonymized Videoc
has bodyop
some
Acoustic Annotation Bodyc
has targetop
some
Segment of Storyc
is super class of
Amplitude Annotationc,
Frequency Annotationc,
F0 Annotationc
label
Acoustic Annotation
en
Title
Acoustic Annotation
en

@prefix : <http://www.purl.org/storytel/> .
@prefix crmdig: <http://www.cidoc-crm.org/extensions/crmdig/> .
@prefix dc: <http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/> .
@prefix oa: <http://www.w3.org/ns/oa#> .
@prefix owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#> .
@prefix rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#> .

: {
    :Acoustic_Annotation a owl:Class ;
        rdfs:label "Acoustic Annotation"@en ;
        dc:title "Acoustic Annotation"@en ;
        rdfs:comment "Annotation holding the result of an acoustic analysis (Praat) of a segment of the Story. It is computed on the original, non-anonymized audio, so it may contain speaker-identifying information and should be handled according to the project data-protection policy."@en ;
        rdfs:isDefinedBy : ;
        rdfs:subClassOf _:nbe3a5c879a974bcb99a97f5216d30ae4b4,
            _:nbe3a5c879a974bcb99a97f5216d30ae4b5,
            _:nbe3a5c879a974bcb99a97f5216d30ae4b6,
            :Annotation ;
        owl:disjointWith :Textual_Annotation .

    _:nbe3a5c879a974bcb99a97f5216d30ae4b4 a owl:Restriction ;
        owl:onProperty crmdig:L43_annotates ;
        owl:someValuesFrom :Story .

    _:nbe3a5c879a974bcb99a97f5216d30ae4b5 a owl:Restriction ;
        owl:onProperty oa:hasBody ;
        owl:someValuesFrom :Acoustic_Annotation_Body .

    _:nbe3a5c879a974bcb99a97f5216d30ae4b6 a owl:Restriction ;
        owl:onProperty oa:hasTarget ;
        owl:someValuesFrom :Story_Segment .
}

Acoustic Annotation Body
c

back to ToC or Class ToC

IRI: http://www.purl.org/storytel/Acoustic_Annotation_Body

comment
Output of the acoustic analysis of a segment of the Story, stored as a digital object (e.g. an F0 data file or a waveform or spectrogram image) and not as text. Because it is computed on the non-anonymized voice it may allow speaker identification.
en
is defined by
http://www.purl.org/storytel/
is sub class of
d1 digital objectc,
Annotation Bodyc
label
Acoustic Annotation Body
en
Title
Acoustic Annotation Body
en

@prefix : <http://www.purl.org/storytel/> .
@prefix crmdig: <http://www.cidoc-crm.org/extensions/crmdig/> .
@prefix dc: <http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/> .
@prefix owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#> .
@prefix rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#> .

: {
    :Acoustic_Annotation_Body a owl:Class ;
        rdfs:label "Acoustic Annotation Body"@en ;
        dc:title "Acoustic Annotation Body"@en ;
        rdfs:comment "Output of the acoustic analysis of a segment of the Story, stored as a digital object (e.g. an F0 data file or a waveform or spectrogram image) and not as text. Because it is computed on the non-anonymized voice it may allow speaker identification."@en ;
        rdfs:isDefinedBy : ;
        rdfs:subClassOf crmdig:D1_Digital_Object,
            :Annotation_Body .
}

Age of the Participants
c

back to ToC or Class ToC

IRI: http://www.purl.org/storytel/Age_Of_The_Participants

comment
Dimension expressing the age of the participants in a group, expressed as an integer value between 18 and 65.
en
is defined by
http://www.purl.org/storytel/
is sub class of
e54 dimensionc
p90 has valueop
some <= restricted by 65
